    "english": "Again consider this same situation. Even though it is not necessarily true that the valid perceptions are identical, it is possible for there to be three valid perceptions here which happen to be identical. This is because, as we have already established logically, there can be a case where by the force of karma three different objects, each one confirmed by a valid perception, start to exist. And since this is possible, then it is equally possible that, by the force of karma, three equivalent valid perceptions of a vessel could start to exist as well."

    "english": "Generally speaking, each of the three objects mentioned--the pus and the other two--are things of the type that block other objects from entering into the space which they themselves occupy. It is no contradiction though to say that, in this situation where the beings are looking this way, they are not objects such that they block other things from entering into the space they occupy. This is true for the following reason. A central mountain of the world which is square in shape, and a central mountain of the world which is round in shape, and the like, are objects such that they block other things from entering the space they occupy. Nevertheless, it is possible for both these things to occupy the space taken up by a single central mountain of the world. A red-hot ball of steel is something that's hot, but consider what happens when a person touches it after he has used the mantra of steel on his hand. The sensation that he feels is not a sensation of heat; on the contrary, it is a sensation of something not heat. [This concludes the section of the text entitled \"The Stream.\"] 128 The Asian Classics Institute Course V: How Karma Works Reading Seven: Black and White Deeds, the \"Path of Action,\" and the Root and Branch Non-Virtues"

And certain kinds of near-gods too would have to see weapons as glasses of water, and on and on; the problems raised would be many."

===== tibetan/313/report.md =====
C05:313 — Tibetan-first provisional proposal by Codex; source origin 8575edf1e0de695677194c1fde60f1d9e2c2f204 through309, distinct from future through312 acceptance.
Challenge first: skye ba → “start to exist” is an arising paraphrase; yod par contributes nearby existential/modality structure and remains omitted. Exact final ranges are Wylie 244:251, English 535:549.
Spans 32; non-null d5 word pairs 26; total d5 26; d7 2; nulls 0; proposed errata 0.
Every pairing, source function, occurrence, parent/member range and complete unwrapped range is in decisions.json. Distributed content is omitted with reasons, without null claims.
Complete original source and physical bytes, full master records, full corpus results and all five errata grounds are under ../evidence/; see five-ground-errata-review.json and all-pins.json. Digital equality proves neither publication independence nor ingestion lineage.
Canonical generator actual EXIT=0; stderr is empty (0 bytes); stdout and body.html are identical (4980 bytes). Exact stdin/stdout/stderr/exit and command metadata are in native/.
spec.json SHA256 9fc44b6e36b4d9a6bd46df2665d0943b58f2cd93cbd5ff0ad108547f7bcd9540; body.html SHA256 e2bd81ae4f2332e810ff8b0157c9b58c194758e8f5f53f064685b61496083677.
native-proof.json proves both complete source strings and every span recovered verbatim, original-builder parsing of every span, and ACIP round trips. Three unchanged pinned gates pass: broken words, split syllables, degenerate members.
The gates count 89 leaf spans across three pages; specs contain 93 total spans including four compound parents. These mechanical results do not establish semantic acceptance.
Registered following-heading apparatus is preserved where present. Row 314 requires the two documented la sogs pa supplied-head licensors at integration; see ../evidence/proposal-licensors.json.
The failed preparation count assumption is retained in ../evidence/prepare-attempt1.* and attempt-history.json; every native generation passed on its first invocation.
Final integrity boundary: ../FINAL-FREEZE.manifest.json, schema codex-independent-proposal-freeze-v1; its digest is in ../FINAL-FREEZE.manifest.sha256. No source, repository, glossary or register changes were made.

===== english/313/report.md =====

===== reconciled/313/report.md =====
C05:313 — PROVISIONAL independent reconciliation by Codex. Source/governance/builder origin 8575edf1e0de695677194c1fde60f1d9e2c2f204 through309.
Actual acceptance baseline through312 has not been received or consumed at this source-review boundary; it must be recorded separately without retroactive provenance.
Hardest ruling: final phyir closes the single A yin te … B, conditional C phyir reason; the sole because introduces that two-part English proof. te receives no second causal atom.
Joint nges par + dgos necessity under mi is kept as d3 not necessarily true; it is is excluded. This is a complete propositional recast, without asserting that true alone translates dgos.
The situational phrase and anaphoric conditional also remain d3. Full HGM tshad grub pa supports the compound and two tighter members; original English flat members are recast at d7.
All repeated perceptions, numerals, karmic forces and arising predicates keep their local subjects. yod pa/yod par modality remains distributed and omitted, without null claims.
Both originals were usable: all 32 Tibetan and 31 English spans have dispositions. No source erratum survives the five-ground screen; exact/shared parallels do not prove independent publication or ingestion lineage.
Final counts: 32 spans; 24 non-null d5 word pairs; 24 total d5; 2 d7; 0 nulls; 0 new errata.
Actual canonical generator EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=5198 bytes. body.html is exactly native stdout; spec=5188 bytes.
spec.json SHA256 7cc777096e132eb4ccdf8d80f7188cd6a0eb3a3f580e79cfc834a3d70ee2d866; body.html SHA256 5b5c5a54f0afabced8d1e2b80ce7210f93a2eece54cd79dce892d87c0b473c60.

===== tibetan/314/report.md =====
C05:314 — Tibetan-first provisional proposal by Codex; source origin 8575edf1e0de695677194c1fde60f1d9e2c2f204 through309, distinct from future through312 acceptance.
Challenge first: yod pa → “occupy” uses locative existence at the single mountain site (Wylie 295:301, English 697:703). It is a local recast, not a generalized lexical definition.
Spans 53; non-null d5 word pairs 40; total d5 40; d7 4; nulls 0; proposed errata 0.
Every pairing, source function, occurrence, parent/member range and complete unwrapped range is in decisions.json. Distributed content is omitted with reasons, without null claims.
Complete original source and physical bytes, full master records, full corpus results and all five errata grounds are under ../evidence/; see five-ground-errata-review.json and all-pins.json. Digital equality proves neither publication independence nor ingestion lineage.
Canonical generator actual EXIT=0; stderr is empty (0 bytes); stdout and body.html are identical (8343 bytes). Exact stdin/stdout/stderr/exit and command metadata are in native/.
spec.json SHA256 3226d02ad52ae8df455f28991e24346671ff940df657087682a67deaf3e3e7f0; body.html SHA256 482eadb98a287dd2a1998939dfe9586effc791ad629b4e22ee83690e36df81cb.
native-proof.json proves both complete source strings and every span recovered verbatim, original-builder parsing of every span, and ACIP round trips. Three unchanged pinned gates pass: broken words, split syllables, degenerate members.
The gates count 89 leaf spans across three pages; specs contain 93 total spans including four compound parents. These mechanical results do not establish semantic acceptance.
Registered following-heading apparatus is preserved where present. Row 314 requires the two documented la sogs pa supplied-head licensors at integration; see ../evidence/proposal-licensors.json.
The failed preparation count assumption is retained in ../evidence/prepare-attempt1.* and attempt-history.json; every native generation passed on its first invocation.
Final integrity boundary: ../FINAL-FREEZE.manifest.json, schema codex-independent-proposal-freeze-v1; its digest is in ../FINAL-FREEZE.manifest.sha256. No source, repository, glossary or register changes were made.

C05:314 — independent English-first PROVISIONAL proposal by Codex; source origin 8575edf1e0de695677194c1fde60f1d9e2c2f204 through309, not future through312 acceptance.

===== reconciled/314/report.md =====

Hardest ruling: omit Tibetan w32 yod pa→occupy. Occupy recasts locative na plus existential yod pa in the shared-space relation; neither gets a false null. The six surrounding useful lexical atoms remain.
Both la sogs pa closing gestures are retained: and the other excludes supplied two; and the like preserves enumeration. Exact proposed allowances and source ranges are in ../evidence/proposed-licensors.json.

Two source mountain names select English occurrences one and three. Three block and sensation occurrences stay in their own clauses. Full original Stream/Reading Seven apparatus stays unaligned.

===== tibetan/315/report.md =====
C05:315 — Tibetan-first provisional proposal by Codex; source origin 8575edf1e0de695677194c1fde60f1d9e2c2f204 through309, distinct from future through312 acceptance.
Challenge first: the full author epithet is retained as one established named identity (Wylie 0:22, English 116:169), supported by the complete HGM record. brtsoms pa is omitted, never falsely matched to selections.
Spans 8; non-null d5 word pairs 4; total d5 4; d7 3; nulls 0; proposed errata 0.
Every pairing, source function, occurrence, parent/member range and complete unwrapped range is in decisions.json. Distributed content is omitted with reasons, without null claims.
Complete original source and physical bytes, full master records, full corpus results and all five errata grounds are under ../evidence/; see five-ground-errata-review.json and all-pins.json. Digital equality proves neither publication independence nor ingestion lineage.
Canonical generator actual EXIT=0; stderr is empty (0 bytes); stdout and body.html are identical (2127 bytes). Exact stdin/stdout/stderr/exit and command metadata are in native/.
spec.json SHA256 5c3ce83f51fe7d753a1636f4c53550c70a3dd9e8b47efea8f707adb20d2e7143; body.html SHA256 823727bb5242014e0bb231ec252bc04bf904cda4268846355ea9b7e61367ac67.
native-proof.json proves both complete source strings and every span recovered verbatim, original-builder parsing of every span, and ACIP round trips. Three unchanged pinned gates pass: broken words, split syllables, degenerate members.
The gates count 89 leaf spans across three pages; specs contain 93 total spans including four compound parents. These mechanical results do not establish semantic acceptance.
Registered following-heading apparatus is preserved where present. Row 314 requires the two documented la sogs pa supplied-head licensors at integration; see ../evidence/proposal-licensors.json.
The failed preparation count assumption is retained in ../evidence/prepare-attempt1.* and attempt-history.json; every native generation passed on its first invocation.
Final integrity boundary: ../FINAL-FREEZE.manifest.json, schema codex-independent-proposal-freeze-v1; its digest is in ../FINAL-FREEZE.manifest.sha256. No source, repository, glossary or register changes were made.
